In *MTG Arena*, our dear friend Sparky introduces you to the game and provides you with some sweet starter decks. We've had some players ask for official decklists, and with update 0.13 coming out next week containing a revamped onboarding experience, the timing couldn't be more perfect!

# Monocolor Decks

Note: The white deck, "Tactical Assault," contains cards unique to *MTG Arena*. These cards (shown below) are legal for Standard only within *MTG Arena*.

![Angelic Reward](https://media.wizards.com/2019/images/daily/1QN8eLtYwP.png)![Confront the Assault](https://media.wizards.com/2019/images/daily/aLU96ppy6H.png)![Inspiring Commander](https://media.wizards.com/2019/images/daily/OQ0vvuXsMC.png)

![Spiritual Guardian](https://media.wizards.com/2019/images/daily/NdKRMXl6n9.png)![Tactical Advantage](https://media.wizards.com/2019/images/daily/OOsHV6EzhU.png)

* * *

Maybe you want to practice before your next Friday Night *Magic*. Or maybe you want to introduce your (totally out of the loop) friend to *Magic: The Gathering* for the first time. Or maybe you are just absolutely, totally in love with a [turn-three Gigantosaurus](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tpOySUK3Vdc). Whatever your motivation for playing *MTG Arena*, these are the perfect decks to get you started.
